&lt;project_context>
# 项目: schema_semantic_constrained_ie

## 运行环境
- 服务器名: westd-16980
- SSH 连接命令: ssh connect.westd.seetacloud.com  # 必须用此命令，~/.ssh/config 自动应用密钥和跳板机，禁止用裸 IP
- 项目代码根目录: /root/schema_semantic_constrained_ie


## 环境搭建
连接服务器后先调 `detect_image` 检测是否为预置镜像。如果匹配，跳过基础环境搭建，只装项目特有依赖。已预装的包不要重复安装。

## 输出风格
回复简洁直接。只输出执行结果和必要信息，不要解释过程、不要客套、不要给未被要求的建议。
&lt;/project_context>
## 任务 B：Llama SciERC Beam Search 551 docs

### 目标
在 westd-16980 cuda:0 上用 Llama 模型 + beam search (beam_size=4) + full_s1 constraints 跑 SciERC 全部 551 docs。

### 服务器
- **westd-16980**，使用 **cuda:0**
- 项目代码路径：`/root/schema_semantic_constrained_ie`
- 环境激活：`source /root/miniconda3/bin/activate`（如需要）

### 模型选择
优先使用 ZS（zero-shot）Llama 模型。检查顺序：
1. `/root/autodl-tmp/models/` 下是否有 Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct 或类似的 base/instruct 模型
2. 如果没有 ZS 模型，用 FT（LoRA merged）模型：`/root/autodl-tmp/checkpoints/llama_lora_scierc_merged`
3. 确认用的是哪个模型并在最终结果中标注 "ZS" 或 "FT"

### 背景
Beam search 代码已在此服务器上实现并验证（之前跑过 Qwen SciERC 551 docs beam search）：
- `compiler/beam_aware_processor.py` — per-beam state 隔离
- `compiler/beam_grammar_processor.py` — XGrammar replay-from-scratch
- `run_constrained_extraction.py` — 支持 `--decoding_strategy beam --num_beams 4`

之前 Qwen 551-doc 结果：DR=0%, CF=0.53%, E_F1=32.00, R_F1=8.56

### 执行步骤

**1. 确认模型路径和 beam search 代码**
```bash
ls /root/autodl-tmp/models/ | grep -i llama
ls /root/autodl-tmp/checkpoints/llama_lora_scierc_merged/
ls /root/schema_semantic_constrained_ie/compiler/beam_aware_processor.py
```

**2. 查看之前 Llama 实验的运行命令**
参考 bash history 或之前 Llama SciERC 的 greedy 命令（可能需要 --model_path 参数）：
```bash
history | grep -i llama | grep scierc | tail -20
```

**3. 跑 beam search 551 docs**
```bash
cd /root/schema_semantic_constrained_ie
python run_constrained_extraction.py \
    --decoding_strategy beam \
    --num_beams 4 \
    --constraint_mode full_s1 \
    --model_path [LLAMA_PATH] \
    [其他参数与 Llama greedy 一致]
```

**重要**：这个任务预计运行 30-60 分钟。**不要轮询等待**。
- 使用 `submit_training_job` 提交（如果可用），或
- 在 tmux 中启动，记录 PID，然后退出并报告 PID
- 如果你选择前台运行，确保不会因超时被杀

**4. 如果成功完成（前台跑完或确认输出 551 行）**
跑 violation_checker + F1 eval，汇报：
```
Model: Llama-3.1-8B [ZS/FT]
Dataset: SciERC (551 docs)
Decoding: beam_size=4, full_s1

ET%: ?
RV%: ?
DR%: ?
CF%: ?
E_F1: ?
R_F1: ?
Parse failures: ?
```

**5. 如果在后台启动**
汇报 PID + tmux session name + 输出文件路径，以便后续收集。

### 注意
- 不要紧密轮询（之前 Workers 因此失败）
- 如果 OOM，尝试 --num_beams 2
- 输出保存到 `outputs/` 下，文件名包含 "llama" 和 "beam"

Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps.
